{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01JQSnpvzZDrYFjHaobFToBa","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01WoEh93SA7RpLYvKtWX3S51","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/pid-control-systems.md","content":"# PID Control Systems for ACC Applications\n\n## Overview\nPID (Proportional-Integral-Derivative) controllers are fundamental for vehicle dynamics control. This skill covers implementing and tuning PID controllers for Adaptive Cruise Control systems.\n\n## Fundamentals\n\n### PID Formula\n```\nu(t) = Kp*e(t) + Ki*∫e(t)dt + Kd*de(t)/dt\n```\n\nWhere:\n- **Kp** (Proportional gain): Responds to current error - affects steady-state error and responsiveness\n- **Ki** (Integral gain): Removes steady-state error over time - can cause oscillation if too high\n- **Kd** (Derivative gain): Dampens overshoot by responding to error rate of change\n\n### Discrete Implementation\nFor real-time control with fixed timestep `dt`:\n\n```python\n# Error tracking\nerror = setpoint - current_value\nerror_integral += error * dt\nerror_derivative = (error - prev_error) / dt\n\n# Control output\noutput = Kp*error + Ki*error_integral + Kd*error_derivative\n\n# Update for next iteration\nprev_error = error\n```\n\n## ACC-Specific Tuning Strategy\n\n### Speed Control Loop\n- **Setpoint**: Target cruise speed (30 m/s)\n- **Feedback**: Ego vehicle speed from speedometer\n- **Output**: Acceleration command\n- **Constraints**: Acceleration limits [-8.0, 3.0] m/s²\n\n**Tuning approach**:\n1. Start with high Kp (2-4) to achieve rise time < 10s\n2. Increase Kd (0.5-2) to control overshoot < 5%\n3. Add small Ki (0.1-0.5) to eliminate steady-state error < 0.5 m/s\n\n### Distance Control Loop\n- **Setpoint**: Safe following distance = lead_speed * time_headway + minimum_gap\n- **Feedback**: Current distance to lead vehicle\n- **Output**: Acceleration command\n- **Constraints**: Must maintain minimum distance > 5m\n\n**Tuning approach**:\n1. Use moderate Kp (1-2) for stability\n2. Lower Kd (0.1-0.5) than speed control to avoid jerky distance corrections\n3. Include Ki (0.05-0.2) for steady-state distance error < 2m\n\n## Implementation Pattern\n\n```python\nclass PIDController:\n    def __init__(self, kp, ki, kd):\n        self.kp = kp\n        self.ki = ki\n        self.kd = kd\n        self.integral = 0.0\n        self.prev_error = 0.0\n\n    def reset(self):\n        self.integral = 0.0\n        self.prev_error = 0.0\n\n    def compute(self, error, dt):\n        # Proportional term\n        p_term = self.kp * error\n\n        # Integral term with anti-windup\n        self.integral += error * dt\n        self.integral = max(-5.0, min(5.0, self.integral))  # Clamp\n        i_term = self.ki * self.integral\n\n        # Derivative term\n        if dt > 0:\n            d_term = self.kd * (error - self.prev_error) / dt\n        else:\n            d_term = 0.0\n\n        # Output\n        output = p_term + i_term + d_term\n\n        # Update state\n        self.prev_error = error\n\n        return output\n```\n\n## Tuning Methods\n\n### Manual Tuning (Ziegler-Nichols variant)\n1. Set Ki=0, Kd=0\n2. Increase Kp until oscillation (find critical gain Kc)\n3. Decrease Kp to 0.45*Kc\n4. Set Ki = 1.2*Kp/Tn (where Tn is oscillation period)\n5. Set Kd = 0.075*Kc*Tn\n\n### Simulation-Based Tuning\n1. Define cost function: `J = ∫(error² + λ*control²)dt`\n2. Test parameter ranges systematically\n3. Evaluate against ACC targets:\n   - Rise time < 10s\n   - Overshoot < 5%\n   - Steady-state error < threshold\n\n## Anti-Windup Techniques\n\n**Integral windup** occurs when integral term grows unbounded due to saturation:\n\n```python\n# Method 1: Integral clamping\nself.integral = max(int_min, min(int_max, self.integral))\n\n# Method 2: Conditional integration\nif abs(output) < max_output:  # Not saturated\n    self.integral += error * dt\n\n# Method 3: Derivative on measurement (avoids derivative kick)\n# Use filtered derivative: d_term = Kd * (setpoint_deriv - actual_deriv)\n```\n\n## Performance Metrics\n\n```python\ndef calculate_rise_time(time_array, response, initial, final):\n    \"\"\"Time to reach 90% of final value\"\"\"\n    threshold = initial + 0.9 * (final - initial)\n    idx = np.where(response >= threshold)[0]\n    return time_array[idx[0]] if len(idx) > 0 else np.nan\n\ndef calculate_overshoot(response, final):\n    \"\"\"Max overshoot percentage\"\"\"\n    max_val = np.max(response)\n    return 100 * (max_val - final) / final if final != 0 else 0\n\ndef calculate_sse(response, final):\n    \"\"\"Steady-state error (last 10% of simulation)\"\"\"\n    return np.abs(response[-int(len(response)*0.1):].mean() - final)\n```\n\n## Key Parameters for This Project\n\n| Parameter | Value | Purpose |\n|-----------|-------|---------|\n| dt | 0.1s | Control loop timestep |\n| Target speed | 30 m/s | Cruise control setpoint |\n| Accel limits | [-8.0, 3.0] m/s² | Vehicle actuator constraints |\n| Time headway | 1.5s | Safe following distance multiplier |\n| Min gap | 10.0m | Minimum spacing between vehicles |\n| Rise time target | < 10s | Speed ramp-up performance |\n| Overshoot target | < 5% | Stability requirement |\n| SSE speed | < 0.5 m/s | Cruise accuracy |\n| SSE distance | < 2m | Following accuracy |\n\n## Testing Strategy\n\n1. **Speed control isolation**: Test with no lead vehicle (cruise mode)\n2. **Distance control isolation**: Test with stationary lead vehicle\n3. **Combined control**: Test with dynamic lead vehicle from sensor_data.csv\n4. **Edge cases**: Lead vehicle acceleration/deceleration, TTC threshold\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":5,"cache_creation_input_tokens":122,"cache_read_input_tokens":19428,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":122,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"82978602-cc27-4177-8dda-5c1737c914cb","uuid":"510ac9a7-d4b9-40ef-9ee4-864c54726e18"}

This skill covers reading, writing, and structuring YAML files for vehicle parameters, ACC settings, and PID tuning results.\n\n## YAML Syntax Basics\n\n```yaml\n# Comments start with #\n\n# Scalar values\nstring_value: \"hello\"\ninteger_value: 42\nfloat_value: 3.14\nboolean_value: true\n\n# Lists\nsimple_list:\n  - item1\n  - item2\n  - item3\n\n# Nested dictionaries\nvehicle:\n  mass: 1500  # kg\n  max_accel: 3.0\n  max_decel: -8.0\n\nnested_structure:\n  acc_settings:\n    set_speed: 30\n    time_headway: 1.5\n    min_gap: 10.0\n  vehicle:\n    mass: 1500\n    wheelbase: 2.7\n```\n\n## Python PyYAML Library\n\n### Installation\n```bash\npip install pyyaml\n```\n\n### Reading YAML Files\n\n```python\nimport yaml\n\n# Basic read\nwith open('config.yaml', 'r') as f:\n    config = yaml.safe_load(f)\n\n# Access nested values\nset_speed = config['acc_settings']['set_speed']\nkp = config['pid_speed']['kp']\n\n# With error handling\ntry:\n    with open('tuning_results.yaml', 'r') as f:\n        tuning = yaml.safe_load(f)\nexcept FileNotFoundError:\n    print(\"File not found\")\nexcept yaml.YAMLError as e:\n    print(f\"YAML error: {e}\")\n```\n\n### Writing YAML Files\n\n```python\nimport yaml\n\n# Dictionary to save\nresults = {\n    'pid_speed': {\n        'kp': 2.5,\n        'ki': 0.3,\n        'kd': 0.8\n    },\n    'pid_distance': {\n        'kp': 1.2,\n        'ki': 0.1,\n        'kd': 0.4\n    },\n    'tuning_date': '2026-01-29',\n    'targets_met': True\n}\n\n# Write with custom formatting\nwith open('tuning_results.yaml', 'w') as f:\n    yaml.dump(results, f,\n              default_flow_style=False,  # Use block style (readable)\n              sort_keys=False,            # Preserve order\n              allow_unicode=True)\n\n# Result:\n# pid_speed:\n#   kp: 2.5\n#   ki: 0.3\n#   kd: 0.8\n# pid_distance:\n#   kp: 1.2\n#   ki: 0.1\n#   kd: 0.4\n```\n\n## Configuration Structure for Vehicle_Params.yaml\n\nRecommended structure for ACC system configuration:\n\n```yaml\n# vehicle_params.yaml structure\n\nvehicle:\n  mass: 1500              # kg\n  wheelbase: 2.7          # m\n  max_acceleration: 3.0   # m/s^2\n  max_deceleration: -8.0  # m/s^2\n\nacc_settings:\n  set_speed: 30           # m/s\n  time_headway: 1.5       # seconds\n  min_gap: 10.0           # meters\n  emergency_ttc: 3.0      # seconds\n  min_distance: 5.0       # meters\n\ncontrol:\n  timestep: 0.1           # seconds\n  simulation_duration: 150 # seconds\n\n# Optional: Initial PID gains (can be overridden by tuning_results.yaml)\npid_defaults:\n  speed:\n    kp: 2.0\n    ki: 0.2\n    kd: 0.5\n  distance:\n    kp: 1.0\n    ki: 0.1\n    kd: 0.2\n```\n\n## Tuning Results Structure\n\n```yaml\n# tuning_results.yaml - stores optimized PID parameters\n\npid_speed:\n  kp: 2.5\n  ki: 0.3\n  kd: 0.8\n\npid_distance:\n  kp: 1.2\n  ki: 0.1\n  kd: 0.4\n\ntuning_metrics:\n  speed_rise_time: 8.5    # seconds\n  speed_overshoot: 2.3    # percent\n  speed_sse: 0.3          # m/s\n  distance_sse: 1.5       # meters\n  tuning_date: \"2026-01-29\"\n  tuning_method: \"simulation_based\"\n```\n\n## Implementation Pattern\n\n```python\nimport yaml\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nclass ConfigManager:\n    \"\"\"Manage vehicle configuration and PID parameters\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config_file='vehicle_params.yaml'):\n        self.config_file = config_file\n        self.config = self.load_config()\n        self.tuning_results = self.load_tuning_results()\n\n    def load_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Load vehicle and ACC parameters\"\"\"\n        try:\n            with open(self.config_file, 'r') as f:\n                return yaml.safe_load(f)\n        except FileNotFoundError:\n            raise FileNotFoundError(f\"Config file {self.config_file} not found\")\n\n    def load_tuning_results(self):\n        \"\"\"Load PID gains from tuning results\"\"\"\n        tuning_file = 'tuning_results.yaml'\n        if Path(tuning_file).exists():\n            with open(tuning_file, 'r') as f:\n                return yaml.safe_load(f)\n        return None\n\n    def get_pid_gains(self, loop_name):\n        \"\"\"Get PID gains for speed or distance control\n\n        Args:\n            loop_name: 'speed' or 'distance'\n\n        Returns:\n            dict with 'kp', 'ki', 'kd' keys\n        \"\"\"\n        if self.tuning_results:\n            return self.tuning_results[f'pid_{loop_name}']\n        return self.config['pid_defaults'][loop_name]\n\n    def save_tuning_results(self, pid_speed, pid_distance, metrics=None):\n        \"\"\"Save tuning results to YAML\"\"\"\n        results = {\n            'pid_speed': pid_speed,\n            'pid_distance': pid_distance\n        }\n        if metrics:\n            results['tuning_metrics'] = metrics\n\n        with open('tuning_results.yaml', 'w') as f:\n            yaml.dump(results, f, default_flow_style=False)\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n### 1. Use Relative Paths Carefully\n```python\n# Good: Use pathlib for cross-platform compatibility\nfrom pathlib import Path\nconfig_path = Path(__file__).parent / 'vehicle_params.yaml'\n\n# Less ideal: Hardcoded relative paths\nconfig_path = 'vehicle_params.yaml'\n```\n\n### 2. Validate Configuration\n```python\ndef validate_config(config):\n    \"\"\"Ensure all required keys exist\"\"\"\n    required_keys = ['vehicle', 'acc_settings', 'control']\n    for key in required_keys:\n        if key not in config:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Missing required config section: {key}\")\n\n    # Validate value ranges\n    if config['vehicle']['max_acceleration'] <= 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"max_acceleration must be positive\")\n    if config['vehicle']['max_deceleration'] >= 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"max_deceleration must be negative\")\n```\n\n### 3. Handle Type Conversions\n```python\n# YAML may interpret values as strings, ensure proper types\nkp = float(config['pid_speed']['kp'])\ntimestep = float(config['control']['timestep'])\n```\n\n### 4. Document Configuration Schema\n```yaml\n# Add comments explaining each parameter\nvehicle:\n  mass: 1500              # kg - Total vehicle mass\n  wheelbase: 2.7          # m - Distance between front and rear axles\n  max_acceleration: 3.0   # m/s^2 - Maximum positive acceleration\n  max_deceleration: -8.0  # m/s^2 - Maximum negative acceleration (deceleration)\n```\n\n## Comparison: YAML vs JSON vs INI\n\n| Feature | YAML | JSON | INI |\n|---------|------|------|-----|\n| Readability | Excellent | Good | Fair |\n| Comments | Yes | No | Yes |\n| Nested structures | Native | Native | Limited |\n| Lists | Native | Native | Limited |\n| Python overhead | safe_load | json.load | configparser |\n| Automotive configs | Ideal | Good | Poor |\n\n## Working with Multiple Configurations\n\n```python\nclass MultiConfigManager:\n    \"\"\"Manage multiple configuration files\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.vehicle_params = self.load_yaml('vehicle_params.yaml')\n        self.tuning_results = self.load_yaml('tuning_results.yaml', required=False)\n        self.sensor_metadata = self.load_yaml('sensor_metadata.yaml', required=False)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def load_yaml(filepath, required=True):\n        try:\n            with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n                return yaml.safe_load(f)\n        except FileNotFoundError:\n            if required:\n                raise\n            return {}\n\n    def merge_configs(self):\n        \"\"\"Merge vehicle params with tuning results\"\"\"\n        config = self.vehicle_params.copy()\n        if self.tuning_results:\n            config['pid_tuned'] = self.tuning_results\n        return config\n```\n\n## Key Takeaways\n\n1. **PyYAML** provides clean, readable config file handling\n2. Cruise Mode\nWhen no lead vehicle detected or far ahead:\n- **Objective**: Maintain set speed (30 m/s)\n- **Controller**: Speed PID\n- **Error**: e_speed = v_setpoint - v_ego\n- **Action**: Ramp up to set speed, maintain cruise\n\n```python\nif lead_vehicle_present and distance < engagement_distance:\n    mode = 'follow'\nelse:\n    mode = 'cruise'\n```\n\n### 2. Follow Mode\nWhen lead vehicle detected at safe distance:\n- **Objective**: Maintain safe following distance\n- **Controller**: Distance PID (primary), speed PID (secondary)\n- **Safe distance**: `d_safe = v_ego * time_headway + min_gap`\n  - time_headway = 1.5s (reaction time)\n  - min_gap = 10.0m (static spacing)\n- **Error**: `e_distance = d_safe - d_actual`\n\n```python\nif lead_vehicle_present:\n    safe_distance = ego_speed * TIME_HEADWAY + MIN_GAP\n    distance_error = safe_distance - current_distance\n    mode = 'follow'\n```\n\n### 3. Emergency Mode\nWhen TTC (Time-To-Collision) is too short:\n- **Objective**: Maximum safe deceleration\n- **Controller**: Override PID, apply max deceleration\n- **TTC**: `ttc = distance / (ego_speed - lead_speed)` if ego_speed > lead_speed\n- **Threshold**: TTC < 3.0 seconds triggers emergency\n\n```python\ndef calculate_ttc(distance, ego_speed, lead_speed):\n    \"\"\"Calculate time-to-collision\"\"\"\n    relative_speed = ego_speed - lead_speed\n    if relative_speed <= 0.001:  # Not closing gap\n        return float('inf')\n    return distance / relative_speed\n\ndef check_emergency_condition(distance, ego_speed, lead_speed, ttc_threshold=3.0):\n    \"\"\"Determine if emergency braking needed\"\"\"\n    if not lead_vehicle_present:\n        return False\n    ttc = calculate_ttc(distance, ego_speed, lead_speed)\n    return ttc < ttc_threshold\n```\n\n## Safety Constraints and Limits\n\n### Minimum Distance Constraint\n```python\nMIN_DISTANCE = 5.0  # meters - Never allow gap below this\n\ndef enforce_minimum_distance(acceleration, ego_speed, distance, dt):\n    \"\"\"Ensure we never violate minimum distance\"\"\"\n    # Project future distance\n    future_distance = distance + ego_speed * dt\n    if future_distance < MIN_DISTANCE:\n        # Force deceleration to prevent collision\n        return MAX_DECELERATION\n    return acceleration\n```\n\n### Jerk Limiting (optional but realistic)\n```python\nMAX_JERK = 2.0  # m/s³ - Rate of acceleration change\n\ndef limit_jerk(new_accel, prev_accel, dt):\n    \"\"\"Smooth acceleration changes to limit jerk\"\"\"\n    max_accel_change = MAX_JERK * dt\n    accel_change = new_accel - prev_accel\n    if abs(accel_change) > max_accel_change:\n        accel_change = max_accel_change * (1 if accel_change > 0 else -1)\n    return prev_accel + accel_change\n```\n\n## Performance Metrics for Evaluation\n\n### 1. Speed Control Metrics\n\n**Rise Time** (time to reach 90% of target):\n```python\ndef calculate_rise_time(time_array, speed_array, target_speed):\n    \"\"\"Time for speed to reach 90% of target\"\"\"\n    threshold = 0.9 * target_speed\n    indices = np.where(speed_array >= threshold)[0]\n    if len(indices) == 0:\n        return np.nan\n    return time_array[indices[0]]\n```\n\n**Overshoot** (maximum above target):\n```python\ndef calculate_overshoot(speed_array, target_speed):\n    \"\"\"Peak overshoot as percentage\"\"\"\n    max_speed = np.max(speed_array)\n    if target_speed == 0:\n        return 0\n    overshoot = (max_speed - target_speed) / target_speed * 100\n    return max(0, overshoot)\n```\n\n**Steady-State Error** (final 10% of test):\n```python\ndef calculate_sse(speed_array, target_speed, window_percent=10):\n    \"\"\"Steady-state error over final portion\"\"\"\n    window_size = int(len(speed_array) * (window_percent / 100))\n    final_speed = speed_array[-window_size:].mean()\n    return abs(final_speed - target_speed)\n```\n\n### 2. Distance Control Metrics\n\n**Distance Steady-State Error**:\n```python\ndef calculate_distance_sse(distance_array, target_distance, window_percent=10):\n    \"\"\"How well distance control maintains target gap\"\"\"\n    window_size = int(len(distance_array) * (window_percent / 100))\n    final_distance = distance_array[-window_size:].mean()\n    return abs(final_distance - target_distance)\n```\n\n**Safety Margin**:\n```python\ndef check_safety_margin(distance_array, min_distance=5.0):\n    \"\"\"Verify minimum distance never violated\"\"\"\n    min_observed = np.min(distance_array)\n    violation = min_observed < min_distance\n    margin = min_observed - min_distance\n    return margin, violation\n```\n\n### 3. Comfort Metrics\n\n**Acceleration Statistics**:\n```python\ndef analyze_acceleration(acceleration_array):\n    \"\"\"Evaluate acceleration smoothness\"\"\"\n    jerk = np.diff(acceleration_array)  # Jerk = d(acceleration)/dt\n\n    stats = {\n        'max_accel': np.max(acceleration_array),\n        'min_accel': np.min(acceleration_array),\n        'mean_accel': np.mean(acceleration_array),\n        'max_jerk': np.max(np.abs(jerk)),\n        'rms_jerk': np.sqrt(np.mean(jerk ** 2))\n    }\n    return stats\n```\n\n## Target Specifications for This Project\n\n| Metric | Target | Constraint |\n|--------|--------|-----------|\n| Speed rise time | < 10s | From 0 to 30 m/s |\n| Speed overshoot | < 5% | Should not exceed 31.5 m/s |\n| Speed steady-state error | < 0.5 m/s | Accuracy of cruise control |\n| Distance steady-state error | < 2.0m | Following accuracy |\n| Minimum distance | > 5.0m | Safety constraint |\n| Initial speed | ~0 m/s | Starting condition |\n| Max acceleration | 3.0 m/s² | Upward bound |\n| Max deceleration | -8.0 m/s² | Downward bound |\n| Emergency TTC | < 3.0s | Trigger level |\n| Time headway | 1.5s | Safe following distance multiplier |\n| Minimum gap | 10.0m | Static spacing component |\n| Control duration | 150s | Full simulation time |\n| Time step | 0.1s | Total 1501 samples |\n\n## Implementation Pattern\n\n```python\nclass VehicleController:\n    \"\"\"ACC system with mode management and safety checks\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config):\n        self.config = config\n        # Extract parameters\n        self.set_speed = config['acc_settings']['set_speed']\n        self.time_headway = config['acc_settings']['time_headway']\n        self.min_gap = config['acc_settings']['min_gap']\n        self.max_accel = config['vehicle']['max_acceleration']\n        self.max_decel = config['vehicle']['max_deceleration']\n        self.ttc_threshold = config['acc_settings']['emergency_ttc']\n        self.min_distance = config['acc_settings']['min_distance']\n\n    def compute_safe_distance(self, ego_speed):\n        \"\"\"Calculate required following distance\"\"\"\n        return ego_speed * self.time_headway + self.min_gap\n\n    def calculate_ttc(self, distance, ego_speed, lead_speed):\n        \"\"\"Time to collision\"\"\"\n        rel_speed = ego_speed - lead_speed\n        if rel_speed <= 0:\n            return float('inf')\n        return distance / rel_speed\n\n    def select_mode(self, ego_speed, lead_speed, distance):\n        \"\"\"Determine control mode\"\"\"\n        if lead_speed is None:\n            return 'cruise'\n\n        ttc = self.calculate_ttc(distance, ego_speed, lead_speed)\n        if ttc < self.ttc_threshold or distance < self.min_distance:\n            return 'emergency'\n\n        return 'follow'\n\n    def compute_control(self, ego_speed, lead_speed, distance, dt):\n        \"\"\"Compute acceleration command with safety checks\"\"\"\n        mode = self.select_mode(ego_speed, lead_speed, distance)\n\n        if mode == 'cruise':\n            accel = self.speed_pid.compute(self.set_speed - ego_speed, dt)\n        elif mode == 'follow':\n            safe_dist = self.compute_safe_distance(ego_speed)\n            distance_error = safe_dist - distance\n            accel = self.distance_pid.compute(distance_error, dt)\n        else:  # emergency\n            accel = self.max_decel\n\n        # Apply constraints\n        accel = max(self.max_decel, min(self.max_accel, accel))\n\n        return accel, mode\n```\n\n## Typical ACC Behavior Sequence\n\n1. **Startup (0-10s)**: Accelerate to set speed (cruise mode)\n2. **Cruising (10-50s)**: Maintain 30 m/s (cruise mode)\n3. **Lead vehicle detected (50s)**: Switch to follow mode\n4. **Following (50-120s)**: Maintain safe distance, adapt to lead vehicle\n5. **Lead vehicle accelerates (120-140s)**: Speed up while maintaining gap\n6. **Lead vehicle decelerates (140-150s)**: Slow down, maintain minimum distance\n\n## Key Safety Principles\n\n1. **Fail-safe**: Always check against hard limits\n2. **Monitor**: Track TTC and distance continuously\n3. **Override**: Emergency braking if any threshold breached\n4. **Conservative**: Prefer over-following (too close) rather than collision\n5. **Smooth**: Minimize jerk for passenger comfort\n\n## References\n\n- SAE J3016 (Driving Automation Levels)\n- SOTIF (Functional Safety)\n- Time headway: Standard 1.5-2.0 seconds in automotive\n- Minimum gap: 10.0m is reasonable for highway speeds\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3002,"cache_read_input_tokens":23936,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3002,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"82978602-cc27-4177-8dda-5c1737c914cb","uuid":"038dcce7-ccfb-4e8b-838e-030af66e4b74"}

Speed Control Metrics\n\n**Rise Time** (time to reach 90% of target):\n```python\ndef calculate_rise_time(time_array, speed_array, target_speed):\n    \"\"\"Time for speed to reach 90% of target\"\"\"\n    threshold = 0.9 * target_speed\n    indices = np.where(speed_array >= threshold)[0]\n    if len(indices) == 0:\n        return np.nan\n    return time_array[indices[0]]\n```\n\n**Overshoot** (maximum above target):\n```python\ndef calculate_overshoot(speed_array, target_speed):\n    \"\"\"Peak overshoot as percentage\"\"\"\n    max_speed = np.max(speed_array)\n    if target_speed == 0:\n        return 0\n    overshoot = (max_speed - target_speed) / target_speed * 100\n    return max(0, overshoot)\n```\n\n**Steady-State Error** (final 10% of test):\n```python\ndef calculate_sse(speed_array, target_speed, window_percent=10):\n    \"\"\"Steady-state error over final portion\"\"\"\n    window_size = int(len(speed_array) * (window_percent / 100))\n    final_speed = speed_array[-window_size:].mean()\n    return abs(final_speed - target_speed)\n```\n\n### 2. Distance Control Metrics\n\n**Distance Steady-State Error**:\n```python\ndef calculate_distance_sse(distance_array, target_distance, window_percent=10):\n    \"\"\"How well distance control maintains target gap\"\"\"\n    window_size = int(len(distance_array) * (window_percent / 100))\n    final_distance = distance_array[-window_size:].mean()\n    return abs(final_distance - target_distance)\n```\n\n**Safety Margin**:\n```python\ndef check_safety_margin(distance_array, min_distance=5.0):\n    \"\"\"Verify minimum distance never violated\"\"\"\n    min_observed = np.min(distance_array)\n    violation = min_observed < min_distance\n    margin = min_observed - min_distance\n    return margin, violation\n```\n\n### 3. Comfort Metrics\n\n**Acceleration Statistics**:\n```python\ndef analyze_acceleration(acceleration_array):\n    \"\"\"Evaluate acceleration smoothness\"\"\"\n    jerk = np.diff(acceleration_array)  # Jerk = d(acceleration)/dt\n\n    stats = {\n        'max_accel': np.max(acceleration_array),\n        'min_accel': np.min(acceleration_array),\n        'mean_accel': np.mean(acceleration_array),\n        'max_jerk': np.max(np.abs(jerk)),\n        'rms_jerk': np.sqrt(np.mean(jerk ** 2))\n    }\n    return stats\n```\n\n## Target Specifications for This Project\n\n| Metric | Target | Constraint |\n|--------|--------|-----------|\n| Speed rise time | < 10s | From 0 to 30 m/s |\n| Speed overshoot | < 5% | Should not exceed 31.5 m/s |\n| Speed steady-state error | < 0.5 m/s | Accuracy of cruise control |\n| Distance steady-state error | < 2.0m | Following accuracy |\n| Minimum distance | > 5.0m | Safety constraint |\n| Initial speed | ~0 m/s | Starting condition |\n| Max acceleration | 3.0 m/s² | Upward bound |\n| Max deceleration | -8.0 m/s² | Downward bound |\n| Emergency TTC | < 3.0s | Trigger level |\n| Time headway | 1.5s | Safe following distance multiplier |\n| Minimum gap | 10.0m | Static spacing component |\n| Control duration | 150s | Full simulation time |\n| Time step | 0.1s | Total 1501 samples |\n\n## Implementation Pattern\n\n```python\nclass VehicleController:\n    \"\"\"ACC system with mode management and safety checks\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config):\n        self.config = config\n        # Extract parameters\n        self.set_speed = config['acc_settings']['set_speed']\n        self.time_headway = config['acc_settings']['time_headway']\n        self.min_gap = config['acc_settings']['min_gap']\n        self.max_accel = config['vehicle']['max_acceleration']\n        self.max_decel = config['vehicle']['max_deceleration']\n        self.ttc_threshold = config['acc_settings']['emergency_ttc']\n        self.min_distance = config['acc_settings']['min_distance']\n\n    def compute_safe_distance(self, ego_speed):\n        \"\"\"Calculate required following distance\"\"\"\n        return ego_speed * self.time_headway + self.min_gap\n\n    def calculate_ttc(self, distance, ego_speed, lead_speed):\n        \"\"\"Time to collision\"\"\"\n        rel_speed = ego_speed - lead_speed\n        if rel_speed <= 0:\n            return float('inf')\n        return distance / rel_speed\n\n    def select_mode(self, ego_speed, lead_speed, distance):\n        \"\"\"Determine control mode\"\"\"\n        if lead_speed is None:\n            return 'cruise'\n\n        ttc = self.calculate_ttc(distance, ego_speed, lead_speed)\n        if ttc < self.ttc_threshold or distance < self.min_distance:\n            return 'emergency'\n\n        return 'follow'\n\n    def compute_control(self, ego_speed, lead_speed, distance, dt):\n        \"\"\"Compute acceleration command with safety checks\"\"\"\n        mode = self.select_mode(ego_speed, lead_speed, distance)\n\n        if mode == 'cruise':\n            accel = self.speed_pid.compute(self.set_speed - ego_speed, dt)\n        elif mode == 'follow':\n            safe_dist = self.compute_safe_distance(ego_speed)\n            distance_error = safe_dist - distance\n            accel = self.distance_pid.compute(distance_error, dt)\n        else:  # emergency\n            accel = self.max_decel\n\n        # Apply constraints\n        accel = max(self.max_decel, min(self.max_accel, accel))\n\n        return accel, mode\n```\n\n## Typical ACC Behavior Sequence\n\n1. **Startup (0-10s)**: Accelerate to set speed (cruise mode)\n2. **Cruising (10-50s)**: Maintain 30 m/s (cruise mode)\n3. **Lead vehicle detected (50s)**: Switch to follow mode\n4. **Following (50-120s)**: Maintain safe distance, adapt to lead vehicle\n5. **Lead vehicle accelerates (120-140s)**: Speed up while maintaining gap\n6. **Lead vehicle decelerates (140-150s)**: Slow down, maintain minimum distance\n\n## Key Safety Principles\n\n1. **Fail-safe**: Always check against hard limits\n2. **Monitor**: Track TTC and distance continuously\n3. **Override**: Emergency braking if any threshold breached\n4. **Conservative**: Prefer over-following (too close) rather than collision\n5. **Smooth**: Minimize jerk for passenger comfort\n\n## References\n\n- SAE J3016 (Driving Automation Levels)\n- SOTIF (Functional Safety)\n- Time headway: Standard 1.5-2.0 seconds in automotive\n- Minimum gap: 10.0m is reasonable for highway speeds\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Python Project Structure for Vehicle Simulation\n\n## Overview\nThis skill covers organizing Python code for vehicle simulation projects, including module organization, imports, and packaging conventions.\n\n## Recommended Project Layout\n\n```\nacc_system/\n├── pid_controller.py          # PID control implementation\n├── acc_system.py              # ACC logic and mode management\n├── simulation.py              # Main simulation runner\n├── vehicle_params.yaml        # Configuration file\n├── sensor_data.csv            # Input sensor data\n├── tuning_results.yaml        # PID tuning output\n├── simulation_results.csv     # Simulation output\n├── acc_report.md              # Analysis report\n└── requirements.txt           # Dependencies\n```\n\n## Python Module Organization\n\n### pid_controller.py\n```python\n\"\"\"\nPID Controller Implementation for ACC System\n\nThis module provides a discrete-time PID controller with anti-windup\nand clipping for automotive control applications.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport numpy as np\n\n\nclass PIDController:\n    \"\"\"\n    Discrete-time PID controller with anti-windup.\n\n    Parameters:\n        kp (float): Proportional gain\n        ki (float): Integral gain\n        kd (float): Derivative gain\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, kp, ki, kd):\n        self.kp = float(kp)\n        self.ki = float(ki)\n        self.kd = float(kd)\n        self.reset()\n\n    def reset(self):\n        \"\"\"Reset controller state.\"\"\"\n        self.integral = 0.0\n        self.prev_error = 0.0\n\n    def compute(self, error, dt):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute PID control output.\n\n        Args:\n            error (float): Control error (setpoint - actual)\n            dt (float): Time step in seconds\n\n        Returns:\n            float: Control output (acceleration command)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Proportional term\n        p_term = self.kp * error\n\n        # Integral term with anti-windup (clamping)\n        self.integral += error * dt\n        self.integral = np.clip(self.integral, -5.0, 5.0)\n        i_term = self.ki * self.integral\n\n        # Derivative term\n        if dt > 0:\n            d_term = self.kd * (error - self.prev_error) / dt\n        else:\n            d_term = 0.0\n\n        # Store for next iteration\n        self.prev_error = error\n\n        # Total output\n        return p_term + i_term + d_term\n\n    def __repr__(self):\n        return f\"PIDController(kp={self.kp}, ki={self.ki}, kd={self.kd})\"\n```\n\n### acc_system.py\n```python\n\"\"\"\nAdaptive Cruise Control System\n\nImplements mode selection, distance calculation, and control logic.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport numpy as np\n\n\nclass AdaptiveCruiseControl:\n    \"\"\"\n    ACC system with multiple control modes.\n\n    Modes:\n        - 'cruise': Maintain set speed (no lead vehicle)\n        - 'follow': Maintain safe distance (lead vehicle present)\n        - 'emergency': Maximum deceleration (safety threshold exceeded)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config):\n        \"\"\"\n        Initialize ACC system from configuration.\n\n        Args:\n            config (dict): Nested configuration dict with keys:\n                - vehicle: {max_acceleration, max_deceleration}\n                - acc_settings: {set_speed, time_headway, min_gap,\n                                emergency_ttc, min_distance}\n        \"\"\"\n        self.config = config\n        self._extract_parameters()\n\n    def _extract_parameters(self):\n        \"\"\"Extract and validate parameters from config.\"\"\"\n        vehicle = self.config.get('vehicle', {})\n        acc = self.config.get('acc_settings', {})\n\n        self.set_speed = float(acc.get('set_speed', 30.0))\n        self.time_headway = float(acc.get('time_headway', 1.5))\n        self.min_gap = float(acc.get('min_gap', 10.0))\n        self.max_accel = float(vehicle.get('max_acceleration', 3.0))\n        self.max_decel = float(vehicle.get('max_deceleration', -8.0))\n        self.ttc_threshold = float(acc.get('emergency_ttc', 3.0))\n        self.min_distance = float(acc.get('min_distance', 5.0))\n\n    def compute_safe_distance(self, ego_speed):\n        \"\"\"Calculate target following distance.\"\"\"\n        return ego_speed * self.time_headway + self.min_gap\n\n    def calculate_ttc(self, distance, ego_speed, lead_speed):\n        \"\"\"Calculate time-to-collision if closing gap.\"\"\"\n        rel_speed = ego_speed - lead_speed\n        if rel_speed <= 0.0:\n            return float('inf')\n        return distance / rel_speed\n\n    def select_mode(self, ego_speed, lead_speed, distance):\n        \"\"\"Determine control mode based on state.\"\"\"\n        if lead_speed is None:\n            return 'cruise'\n\n        ttc = self.calculate_ttc(distance, ego_speed, lead_speed)\n        if ttc < self.ttc_threshold or distance < self.min_distance:\n            return 'emergency'\n\n        return 'follow'\n\n    def compute(self, ego_speed, lead_speed, distance, speed_accel,\n                distance_accel, dt):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute ACC control command.\n\n        Args:\n            ego_speed (float): Current vehicle speed (m/s)\n            lead_speed (float): Lead vehicle speed or None\n            distance (float): Distance to lead vehicle or None\n            speed_accel (float): Acceleration from speed PID\n            distance_accel (float): Acceleration from distance PID\n            dt (float): Time step\n\n        Returns:\n            tuple: (acceleration_cmd, mode, distance_error)\n        \"\"\"\n        mode = self.select_mode(ego_speed, lead_speed, distance)\n\n        if mode == 'cruise':\n            accel = speed_accel\n            distance_error = None\n        elif mode == 'follow':\n            safe_dist = self.compute_safe_distance(ego_speed)\n            distance_error = safe_dist - distance\n            # Blend distance and speed control\n            accel = distance_accel\n        else:  # emergency\n            accel = self.max_decel\n            distance_error = None\n\n        # Apply acceleration limits\n        accel = np.clip(accel, self.max_decel, self.max_accel)\n\n        return accel, mode, distance_error\n```\n\n### simulation.py\n```python\n\"\"\"\nACC System Simulation Runner\n\nLoads configuration and sensor data, runs 150-second simulation,\nand generates results.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport yaml\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom pid_controller import PIDController\nfrom acc_system import AdaptiveCruiseControl\n\n\ndef load_config(config_file='vehicle_params.yaml'):\n    \"\"\"Load vehicle configuration from YAML.\"\"\"\n    with open(config_file, 'r') as f:\n        return yaml.safe_load(f)\n\n\ndef load_sensor_data(data_file='sensor_data.csv'):\n    \"\"\"Load sensor data from CSV.\"\"\"\n    return pd.read_csv(data_file)\n\n\ndef load_tuning_results(results_file='tuning_results.yaml'):\n    \"\"\"Load PID tuning results from YAML.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        with open(results_file, 'r') as f:\n            return yaml.safe_load(f)\n    except FileNotFoundError:\n        return None\n\n\ndef get_lead_vehicle_data(sensor_df, current_time):\n    \"\"\"Extract lead vehicle data at given timestamp.\"\"\"\n    idx = int(round(current_time / 0.1))\n    if idx >= len(sensor_df):\n        idx = len(sensor_df) - 1\n\n    row = sensor_df.iloc[idx]\n\n    # Check if lead vehicle data exists\n    if pd.isna(row['lead_speed']) or pd.isna(row['distance']):\n        return None, None\n\n    return float(row['lead_speed']), float(row['distance'])\n\n\ndef run_simulation(config, sensor_df, pid_speed, pid_distance, duration=150.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run 150-second ACC simulation.\n\n    Returns:\n        DataFrame with columns: time, ego_speed, acceleration_cmd, mode,\n                               distance_error, distance, ttc\n    \"\"\"\n    dt = 0.1\n    timesteps = int(duration / dt) + 1\n    time_array = np.linspace(0, duration, timesteps)\n\n    # Initialize arrays\n    ego_speed = np.zeros(timesteps)\n    accel_cmd = np.zeros(timesteps)\n    mode_array = []\n    distance_error_array = []\n    distance_array = np.full(timesteps, np.nan)\n    ttc_array = np.full(timesteps, np.nan)\n\n    # Initialize ACC system\n    acc = AdaptiveCruiseControl(config)\n\n    # Simulation loop\n    for i in range(1, timesteps):\n        current_time = time_array[i]\n\n        # Get lead vehicle data\n        lead_speed, distance = get_lead_vehicle_data(sensor_df, current_time)\n\n        # Compute PID outputs\n        speed_error = config['acc_settings']['set_speed'] - ego_speed[i-1]\n        speed_accel = pid_speed.compute(speed_error, dt)\n\n        if distance is not None:\n            distance_array[i] = distance\n            safe_dist = acc.compute_safe_distance(ego_speed[i-1])\n            distance_error = safe_dist - distance\n            distance_accel = pid_distance.compute(distance_error, dt)\n        else:\n            distance_error = None\n            distance_accel = speed_accel\n\n        # Compute ACC control\n        accel, mode, dist_error = acc.compute(\n            ego_speed[i-1], lead_speed, distance,\n            speed_accel, distance_accel, dt\n        )\n\n        accel_cmd[i] = accel\n        mode_array.append(mode)\n        distance_error_array.append(dist_error)\n\n        # Update speed with kinematics\n        ego_speed[i] = ego_speed[i-1] + accel * dt\n        ego_speed[i] = np.clip(ego_speed[i], 0, 40)  # Clamp speed\n\n        # Calculate TTC if lead vehicle present\n        if distance is not None and ego_speed[i] > lead_speed:\n            rel_speed = ego_speed[i] - lead_speed\n            ttc_array[i] = distance / rel_speed if rel_speed > 0 else float('inf')\n\n    # Construct results DataFrame\n    results = pd.DataFrame({\n        'time': time_array,\n        'ego_speed': ego_speed,\n        'acceleration_cmd': accel_cmd,\n        'mode': mode_array + [mode_array[-1]],  # Pad for length match\n        'distance_error': distance_error_array + [distance_error_array[-1]],\n        'distance': distance_array,\n        'ttc': ttc_array\n    })\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Run complete ACC simulation.\"\"\"\n    # Load configuration and data\n    config = load_config('vehicle_params.yaml')\n    sensor_df = load_sensor_data('sensor_data.csv')\n    tuning_results = load_tuning_results('tuning_results.yaml')\n\n    # Extract PID gains from tuning results\n    if tuning_results:\n        pid_speed_gains = tuning_results['pid_speed']\n        pid_distance_gains = tuning_results['pid_distance']\n    else:\n        # Fallback to defaults\n        pid_speed_gains = config.get('pid_defaults', {}).get('speed', {})\n        pid_distance_gains = config.get('pid_defaults', {}).get('distance', {})\n\n    # Initialize PID controllers\n    pid_speed = PIDController(\n        pid_speed_gains['kp'],\n        pid_speed_gains['ki'],\n        pid_speed_gains['kd']\n    )\n    pid_distance = PIDController(\n        pid_distance_gains['kp'],\n        pid_distance_gains['ki'],\n        pid_distance_gains['kd']\n    )\n\n    # Run simulation\n    print(\"Running 150-second ACC simulation...\")\n    results = run_simulation(config, sensor_df, pid_speed, pid_distance)\n\n    # Save results\n    results.to_csv('simulation_results.csv', index=False, float_format='%.1f')\n    print(f\"Saved {len(results)} rows to simulation_results.csv\")\n\n    # Display summary\n    print(\"\\nSimulation Summary:\")\n    print(f\"  Speed range: {results['ego_speed'].min():.2f} - \"\n          f\"{results['ego_speed'].max():.2f} m/s\")\n    print(f\"  Average acceleration: {results['acceleration_cmd'].mean():.2f} m/s²\")\n    print(f\"  Final speed: {results['ego_speed'].iloc[-1]:.2f} m/s\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n```\n\n## acc_system.py\n```python\n\"\"\"\nAdaptive Cruise Control System\n\nImplements mode selection, distance calculation, and control logic.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport numpy as np\n\n\nclass AdaptiveCruiseControl:\n    \"\"\"\n    ACC system with multiple control modes.\n\n    Modes:\n        - 'cruise': Maintain set speed (no lead vehicle)\n        - 'follow': Maintain safe distance (lead vehicle present)\n        - 'emergency': Maximum deceleration (safety threshold exceeded)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config):\n        \"\"\"\n        Initialize ACC system from configuration.\n\n        Args:\n            config (dict): Nested configuration dict with keys:\n                - vehicle: {max_acceleration, max_deceleration}\n                - acc_settings: {set_speed, time_headway, min_gap,\n                                emergency_ttc, min_distance}\n        \"\"\"\n        self.config = config\n        self._extract_parameters()\n\n    def _extract_parameters(self):\n        \"\"\"Extract and validate parameters from config.\"\"\"\n        vehicle = self.config.get('vehicle', {})\n        acc = self.config.get('acc_settings', {})\n\n        self.set_speed = float(acc.get('set_speed', 30.0))\n        self.time_headway = float(acc.get('time_headway', 1.5))\n        self.min_gap = float(acc.get('min_gap', 10.0))\n        self.max_accel = float(vehicle.get('max_acceleration', 3.0))\n        self.max_decel = float(vehicle.get('max_deceleration', -8.0))\n        self.ttc_threshold = float(acc.get('emergency_ttc', 3.0))\n        self.min_distance = float(acc.get('min_distance', 5.0))\n\n    def compute_safe_distance(self, ego_speed):\n        \"\"\"Calculate target following distance.\"\"\"\n        return ego_speed * self.time_headway + self.min_gap\n\n    def calculate_ttc(self, distance, ego_speed, lead_speed):\n        \"\"\"Calculate time-to-collision if closing gap.\"\"\"\n        rel_speed = ego_speed - lead_speed\n        if rel_speed <= 0.0:\n            return float('inf')\n        return distance / rel_speed\n\n    def select_mode(self, ego_speed, lead_speed, distance):\n        \"\"\"Determine control mode based on state.\"\"\"\n        if lead_speed is None:\n            return 'cruise'\n\n        ttc = self.calculate_ttc(distance, ego_speed, lead_speed)\n        if ttc < self.ttc_threshold or distance < self.min_distance:\n            return 'emergency'\n\n        return 'follow'\n\n    def compute(self, ego_speed, lead_speed, distance, speed_accel,\n                distance_accel, dt):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute ACC control command.\n\n        Args:\n            ego_speed (float): Current vehicle speed (m/s)\n            lead_speed (float): Lead vehicle speed or None\n            distance (float): Distance to lead vehicle or None\n            speed_accel (float): Acceleration from speed PID\n            distance_accel (float): Acceleration from distance PID\n            dt (float): Time step\n\n        Returns:\n            tuple: (acceleration_cmd, mode, distance_error)\n        \"\"\"\n        mode = self.select_mode(ego_speed, lead_speed, distance)\n\n        if mode == 'cruise':\n            accel = speed_accel\n            distance_error = None\n        elif mode == 'follow':\n            safe_dist = self.compute_safe_distance(ego_speed)\n            distance_error = safe_dist - distance\n            # Blend distance and speed control\n            accel = distance_accel\n        else:  # emergency\n            accel = self.max_decel\n            distance_error = None\n\n        # Apply acceleration limits\n        accel = np.clip(accel, self.max_decel, self.max_accel)\n\n        return accel, mode, distance_error\n```\n\n### simulation.py\n```python\n\"\"\"\nACC System Simulation Runner\n\nLoads configuration and sensor data, runs 150-second simulation,\nand generates results.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport yaml\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom pid_controller import PIDController\nfrom acc_system import AdaptiveCruiseControl\n\n\ndef load_config(config_file='vehicle_params.yaml'):\n    \"\"\"Load vehicle configuration from YAML.\"\"\"\n    with open(config_file, 'r') as f:\n        return yaml.safe_load(f)\n\n\ndef load_sensor_data(data_file='sensor_data.csv'):\n    \"\"\"Load sensor data from CSV.\"\"\"\n    return pd.read_csv(data_file)\n\n\ndef load_tuning_results(results_file='tuning_results.yaml'):\n    \"\"\"Load PID tuning results from YAML.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        with open(results_file, 'r') as f:\n            return yaml.safe_load(f)\n    except FileNotFoundError:\n        return None\n\n\ndef get_lead_vehicle_data(sensor_df, current_time):\n    \"\"\"Extract lead vehicle data at given timestamp.\"\"\"\n    idx = int(round(current_time / 0.1))\n    if idx >= len(sensor_df):\n        idx = len(sensor_df) - 1\n\n    row = sensor_df.iloc[idx]\n\n    # Check if lead vehicle data exists\n    if pd.isna(row['lead_speed']) or pd.isna(row['distance']):\n        return None, None\n\n    return float(row['lead_speed']), float(row['distance'])\n\n\ndef run_simulation(config, sensor_df, pid_speed, pid_distance, duration=150.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run 150-second ACC simulation.\n\n    Returns:\n        DataFrame with columns: time, ego_speed, acceleration_cmd, mode,\n                               distance_error, distance, ttc\n    \"\"\"\n    dt = 0.1\n    timesteps = int(duration / dt) + 1\n    time_array = np.linspace(0, duration, timesteps)\n\n    # Initialize arrays\n    ego_speed = np.zeros(timesteps)\n    accel_cmd = np.zeros(timesteps)\n    mode_array = []\n    distance_error_array = []\n    distance_array = np.full(timesteps, np.nan)\n    ttc_array = np.full(timesteps, np.nan)\n\n    # Initialize ACC system\n    acc = AdaptiveCruiseControl(config)\n\n    # Simulation loop\n    for i in range(1, timesteps):\n        current_time = time_array[i]\n\n        # Get lead vehicle data\n        lead_speed, distance = get_lead_vehicle_data(sensor_df, current_time)\n\n        # Compute PID outputs\n        speed_error = config['acc_settings']['set_speed'] - ego_speed[i-1]\n        speed_accel = pid_speed.compute(speed_error, dt)\n\n        if distance is not None:\n            distance_array[i] = distance\n            safe_dist = acc.compute_safe_distance(ego_speed[i-1])\n            distance_error = safe_dist - distance\n            distance_accel = pid_distance.compute(distance_error, dt)\n        else:\n            distance_error = None\n            distance_accel = speed_accel\n\n        # Compute ACC control\n        accel, mode, dist_error = acc.compute(\n            ego_speed[i-1], lead_speed, distance,\n            speed_accel, distance_accel, dt\n        )\n\n        accel_cmd[i] = accel\n        mode_array.append(mode)\n        distance_error_array.append(dist_error)\n\n        # Update speed with kinematics\n        ego_speed[i] = ego_speed[i-1] + accel * dt\n        ego_speed[i] = np.clip(ego_speed[i], 0, 40)  # Clamp speed\n\n        # Calculate TTC if lead vehicle present\n        if distance is not None and ego_speed[i] > lead_speed:\n            rel_speed = ego_speed[i] - lead_speed\n            ttc_array[i] = distance / rel_speed if rel_speed > 0 else float('inf')\n\n    # Construct results DataFrame\n    results = pd.DataFrame({\n        'time': time_array,\n        'ego_speed': ego_speed,\n        'acceleration_cmd': accel_cmd,\n        'mode': mode_array + [mode_array[-1]],  # Pad for length match\n        'distance_error': distance_error_array + [distance_error_array[-1]],\n        'distance': distance_array,\n        'ttc': ttc_array\n    })\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Run complete ACC simulation.\"\"\"\n    # Load configuration and data\n    config = load_config('vehicle_params.yaml')\n    sensor_df = load_sensor_data('sensor_data.csv')\n    tuning_results = load_tuning_results('tuning_results.yaml')\n\n    # Extract PID gains from tuning results\n    if tuning_results:\n        pid_speed_gains = tuning_results['pid_speed']\n        pid_distance_gains = tuning_results['pid_distance']\n    else:\n        # Fallback to defaults\n        pid_speed_gains = config.get('pid_defaults', {}).get('speed', {})\n        pid_distance_gains = config.get('pid_defaults', {}).get('distance', {})\n\n    # Initialize PID controllers\n    pid_speed = PIDController(\n        pid_speed_gains['kp'],\n        pid_speed_gains['ki'],\n        pid_speed_gains['kd']\n    )\n    pid_distance = PIDController(\n        pid_distance_gains['kp'],\n        pid_distance_gains['ki'],\n        pid_distance_gains['kd']\n    )\n\n    # Run simulation\n    print(\"Running 150-second ACC simulation...\")\n    results = run_simulation(config, sensor_df, pid_speed, pid_distance)\n\n    # Save results\n    results.to_csv('simulation_results.csv', index=False, float_format='%.1f')\n    print(f\"Saved {len(results)} rows to simulation_results.csv\")\n\n    # Display summary\n    print(\"\\nSimulation Summary:\")\n    print(f\"  Speed range: {results['ego_speed'].min():.2f} - \"\n          f\"{results['ego_speed'].max():.2f} m/s\")\n    print(f\"  Average acceleration: {results['acceleration_cmd'].mean():.2f} m/s²\")\n    print(f\"  Final speed: {results['ego_speed'].iloc[-1]:.2f} m/s\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n```\n\n## Import Conventions\n\n```python\n# Standard library imports\nimport sys\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# Third-party imports\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\nimport yaml\n\n# Local imports\nfrom pid_controller import PIDController\nfrom acc_system import AdaptiveCruiseControl\n```\n\n## Dependencies (requirements.txt)\n\n```\nnumpy>=1.21.0\npandas>=1.3.0\npyyaml>=5.4.0\n```\n\n## Running the Project\n\n```bash\n# Install dependencies\npip install -r requirements.txt\n\n# Run simulation\npython simulation.py\n\n# Check outputs\nls -l *.csv *.yaml *.md\n```\n\n## Common Issues and Solutions\n\n### 1. Module Import Errors\n```python\n# Add project directory to path\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))\n```\n\n### 2. Configuration Not Found\n```python\ndef find_config():\n    \"\"\"Search for config file in common locations\"\"\"\n    possible_paths = [\n        Path.cwd() / 'vehicle_params.yaml',\n        Path(__file__).parent / 'vehicle_params.yaml',\n        Path.home() / '.acc' / 'vehicle_params.yaml'\n    ]\n    for path in possible_paths:\n        if path.exists():\n            return str(path)\n    raise FileNotFoundError(\"vehicle_params.yaml not found\")\n```\n\n### 3. Large Data File Handling\n```python\n# For simulations with massive datasets, use chunking\ndef process_sensor_data_chunked(filename, chunk_size=100):\n    for chunk in pd.read_csv(filename, chunksize=chunk_size):\n        yield chunk\n```\n\n## Testing Structure\n\n```python\n# test_pid_controller.py\nimport unittest\nfrom pid_controller import PIDController\n\nclass TestPIDController(unittest.TestCase):\n    def setUp(self):\n        self.pid = PIDController(kp=1.0, ki=0.1, kd=0.5)\n\n    def test_proportional_response(self):\n        output = self.pid.compute(error=1.0, dt=0.1)\n        self.assertAlmostEqual(output, 1.0)\n\n    def test_integral_accumulation(self):\n        # Multiple calls accumulate integral\n        self.pid.compute(error=1.0, dt=0.1)\n        self.pid.compute(error=1.0, dt=0.1)\n        output = self.pid.compute(error=1.0, dt=0.1)\n        self.assertGreater(output, 1.0)  # Integral term present\n```\n\n## Logging for Debugging\n\n```python\nimport logging\n\n# Configure logging\nlogging.basicConfig(\n    level=logging.INFO,\n    format='%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s'\n)\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n# In simulation\nlogger.info(f\"Time: {current_time:.1f}s, Mode: {mode}, Speed: {ego_speed:.2f} m/s\")\n```\n\n## Key Takeaways\n\n1. **Modular structure**: Separate concerns (PID, ACC, simulation)\n2. **Clear interfaces**: Well-defined method signatures and return types\n3. **Configuration management**: Use YAML for external parameters\n4. **Documentation**: Docstrings for all classes and methods\n5. **Error handling**: Validate inputs and handle missing files gracefully\n6. **Reproducibility**: Save all inputs and outputs for analysis\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

e(t-1))/dt\n\n    Anti-windup is applied by clamping the integral term to prevent\n    unbounded growth when the output is saturated.\n\n    Parameters:\n        kp (float): Proportional gain (typically 0.5-5.0)\n        ki (float): Integral gain (typically 0.0-2.0)\n        kd (float): Derivative gain (typically 0.0-2.0)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, kp, ki, kd):\n        \"\"\"\n        Initialize PID controller with gains.\n\n        Args:\n            kp (float): Proportional gain\n            ki (float): Integral gain\n            kd (float): Derivative gain\n        \"\"\"\n        self.kp = float(kp)\n        self.ki = float(ki)\n        self.kd = float(kd)\n        self.reset()\n\n    def reset(self):\n        \"\"\"Reset controller state (integral and previous error).\"\"\"\n        self.integral = 0.0\n        self.prev_error = 0.0\n\n    def compute(self, error, dt):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute PID control output.\n\n        Args:\n            error (float): Control error (setpoint - actual value)\n            dt (float): Time step in seconds\n\n        Returns:\n            float: Control output (acceleration command m/s^2)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Proportional term\n        p_term = self.kp * error\n\n        # Integral term with anti-windup\n        # Accumulate error over time\n        self.integral += error * dt\n        # Clamp integral to prevent unbounded growth\n        self.integral = np.clip(self.integral, -5.0, 5.0)\n        i_term = self.ki * self.integral\n\n        # Derivative term\n        # Rate of change of error\n        if dt > 0:\n            d_term = self.kd * (error - self.prev_error) / dt\n        else:\n            d_term = 0.0\n\n        # Store error for next iteration\n        self.prev_error = error\n\n        # Total control output\n        output = p_term + i_term + d_term\n\n        return output\n\n    def __repr__(self):\n        \"\"\"String representation of controller.\"\"\"\n        return f\"PIDController(kp={self.kp:.3f}, ki={self.ki:.3f}, kd={self.kd:.3f})\"\nEOF\ncat /root/pid_controller.py\n","description":"Create pid_controller.py with PIDController class"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":377,"cache_read_input_tokens":37600,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":377,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":26},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"82978602-cc27-4177-8dda-5c1737c914cb","uuid":"f434f2e0-3e90-4808-b154-0d025c4e1053"}

Presence of lead vehicle\n        - Distance to lead vehicle\n        - Time-to-collision (TTC)\n        - Minimum safety distance\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config):\n        \"\"\"\n        Initialize ACC system from configuration dictionary.\n\n        Args:\n            config (dict): Nested configuration dictionary with keys:\n                - vehicle: Contains max_acceleration, max_deceleration\n                - acc_settings: Contains set_speed, time_headway, min_gap,\n                               emergency_ttc, min_distance\n        \"\"\"\n        self.config = config\n        self._extract_parameters()\n\n    def _extract_parameters(self):\n        \"\"\"Extract and validate parameters from configuration.\"\"\"\n        # Vehicle dynamics parameters\n        vehicle = self.config.get('vehicle', {})\n        self.max_accel = float(vehicle.get('max_acceleration', 3.0))\n        self.max_decel = float(vehicle.get('max_deceleration', -8.0))\n\n        # ACC control parameters\n        acc = self.config.get('acc_settings', {})\n        self.set_speed = float(acc.get('set_speed', 30.0))\n        self.time_headway = float(acc.get('time_headway', 1.5))\n        self.min_gap = float(acc.get('min_gap', 10.0))\n        self.ttc_threshold = float(acc.get('emergency_ttc', 3.0))\n        self.min_distance = float(acc.get('min_distance', 5.0))\n\n    def compute_safe_distance(self, ego_speed):\n        \"\"\"\n        Calculate target following distance using time-headway model.\n\n        Formula: d_safe = v * time_headway + min_gap\n\n        Args:\n            ego_speed (float): Current ego vehicle speed (m/s)\n\n        Returns:\n            float: Safe following distance (m)\n        \"\"\"\n        return ego_speed * self.time_headway + self.min_gap\n\n    def calculate_ttc(self, distance, ego_speed, lead_speed):\n        \"\"\"\n        Calculate time-to-collision if gap is closing.\n\n        Formula: TTC = distance / (ego_speed - lead_speed)\n\n        Returns infinity if not closing gap.\n\n        Args:\n            distance (float): Current distance to lead vehicle (m)\n            ego_speed (float): Ego vehicle speed (m/s)\n            lead_speed (float): Lead vehicle speed (m/s)\n\n        Returns:\n            float: Time to collision in seconds, or infinity if not closing\n        \"\"\"\n        relative_speed = ego_speed - lead_speed\n        if relative_speed <= 0.001:  # Not closing gap (avoid division by near-zero)\n            return float('inf')\n        return distance / relative_speed\n\n    def select_mode(self, ego_speed, lead_speed, distance):\n        \"\"\"\n        Select ACC control mode based on current state.\n\n        Mode selection logic:\n        1. If no lead vehicle -> 'cruise'\n        2. If TTC < threshold OR distance < min_distance -> 'emergency'\n        3. Otherwise -> 'follow'\n\n        Args:\n            ego_speed (float): Current ego vehicle speed (m/s)\n            lead_speed (float): Lead vehicle speed (m/s), or None\n            distance (float): Current distance to lead vehicle (m), or None\n\n        Returns:\n            str: Control mode ('cruise', 'follow', or 'emergency')\n        \"\"\"\n        # No lead vehicle - cruise at set speed\n        if lead_speed is None:\n            return 'cruise'\n\n        # Lead vehicle present - check safety conditions\n        ttc = self.calculate_ttc(distance, ego_speed, lead_speed)\n\n        # Emergency braking if critical conditions\n        if ttc < self.ttc_threshold or distance < self.min_distance:\n            return 'emergency'\n\n        # Normal following\n        return 'follow'\n\n    def compute(self, ego_speed, lead_speed, distance, speed_accel,\n                distance_accel, dt):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute ACC control command for current state.\n\n        Selects mode and applies appropriate control strategy:\n        - Cruise: Use speed PID to reach set speed\n        - Follow: Use distance PID to maintain safe gap\n        - Emergency: Override with maximum safe deceleration\n\n        Args:\n            ego_speed (float): Current ego vehicle speed (m/s)\n            lead_speed (float): Lead vehicle speed (m/s), or None\n            distance (float): Current distance to lead vehicle (m), or None\n            speed_accel (float): Acceleration from speed PID (m/s^2)\n            distance_accel (float): Acceleration from distance PID (m/s^2)\n            dt (float): Time step (s)\n\n        Returns:\n            tuple: (acceleration_cmd, mode, distance_error)\n                - acceleration_cmd (float): Clamped acceleration command (m/s^2)\n                - mode (str): Selected control mode\n                - distance_error (float): Distance tracking error, or None\n        \"\"\"\n        # Determine control mode\n        mode = self.select_mode(ego_speed, lead_speed, distance)\n\n        # Compute control output based on mode\n        if mode == 'cruise':\n            # Speed control: accelerate/decelerate to set speed\n            accel = speed_accel\n            distance_error = None\n\n        elif mode == 'follow':\n            # Distance control: maintain safe following distance\n            safe_dist = self.compute_safe_distance(ego_speed)\n            distance_error = safe_dist - distance\n\n            # Use distance PID for following\n            accel = distance_accel\n\n        else:  # mode == 'emergency'\n            # Safety override: maximum safe deceleration\n            accel = self.max_decel\n            distance_error = None\n\n        # Apply acceleration limits (hard constraints)\n        accel = np.clip(accel, self.max_decel, self.max_accel)\n\n        return accel, mode, distance_error\n\n    def __repr__(self):\n        \"\"\"String representation of ACC system.\"\"\"\n        return (f\"AdaptiveCruiseControl(set_speed={self.set_speed} m/s, \"\n                f\"time_headway={self.time_headway}s, min_gap={self.min_gap}m)\")\nEOF\ncat /root/acc_system.py\n","description":"Create acc_system.py with AdaptiveCruiseControl class"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":377,"cache_read_input_tokens":39480,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":377,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"82978602-cc27-4177-8dda-5c1737c914cb","uuid":"b7773b11-1353-45eb-b803-0357b85f26dd"}

1\n\n    row = sensor_df.iloc[idx]\n\n    # Check if lead vehicle data is valid (not NaN)\n    if pd.isna(row['lead_speed']) or pd.isna(row['distance']):\n        return None, None\n\n    return float(row['lead_speed']), float(row['distance'])\n\n\ndef run_simulation(config, sensor_df, pid_speed, pid_distance, duration=150.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run 150-second ACC simulation.\n\n    Args:\n        config: Vehicle configuration dict\n        sensor_df: Sensor data DataFrame\n        pid_speed: PID controller for speed\n        pid_distance: PID controller for distance\n        duration: Simulation duration in seconds\n\n    Returns:\n        DataFrame with simulation results (exactly 1501 rows)\n    \"\"\"\n    dt = 0.1\n    timesteps = int(duration / dt) + 1\n    time_array = np.linspace(0, duration, timesteps)\n\n    # Initialize result arrays\n    ego_speed = np.zeros(timesteps)\n    accel_cmd = np.zeros(timesteps)\n    mode_array = []\n    distance_error_array = []\n    distance_array = np.full(timesteps, np.nan)\n    ttc_array = np.full(timesteps, np.nan)\n\n    # Initialize ACC system\n    acc = AdaptiveCruiseControl(config)\n\n    print(f\"Starting simulation with {timesteps} timesteps\")\n    print(f\"PID Speed: {pid_speed}\")\n    print(f\"PID Distance: {pid_distance}\")\n    print(f\"ACC System: {acc}\")\n\n    # Simulation loop\n    for i in range(1, timesteps):\n        current_time = time_array[i]\n\n        # Get lead vehicle data (or None if not present)\n        lead_speed, distance = get_lead_vehicle_data(sensor_df, current_time)\n\n        # Compute speed PID output (always needed for cruise)\n        speed_error = config['acc_settings']['set_speed'] - ego_speed[i-1]\n        speed_accel = pid_speed.compute(speed_error, dt)\n\n        # Compute distance PID output (if lead vehicle present)\n        if distance is not None:\n            distance_array[i] = distance\n            safe_dist = acc.compute_safe_distance(ego_speed[i-1])\n            distance_error = safe_dist - distance\n            distance_accel = pid_distance.compute(distance_error, dt)\n        else:\n            distance_error = None\n            distance_accel = speed_accel  # Use speed control as fallback\n\n        # Compute ACC control command\n        accel, mode, dist_error = acc.compute(\n            ego_speed[i-1], lead_speed, distance,\n            speed_accel, distance_accel, dt\n        )\n\n        # Store acceleration command\n        accel_cmd[i] = accel\n        mode_array.append(mode)\n        distance_error_array.append(dist_error)\n\n        # Update speed using kinematics: v(t+dt) = v(t) + a(t)*dt\n        ego_speed[i] = ego_speed[i-1] + accel * dt\n\n        # Clamp speed to reasonable range [0, 50] m/s\n        ego_speed[i] = np.clip(ego_speed[i], 0, 50)\n\n        # Calculate TTC if lead vehicle present and closing gap\n        if distance is not None and ego_speed[i] > lead_speed:\n            rel_speed = ego_speed[i] - lead_speed\n            ttc_array[i] = distance / rel_speed if rel_speed > 0 else float('inf')\n\n    # Add first mode (cruise since no lead vehicle at start)\n    mode_array.insert(0, 'cruise')\n    distance_error_array.insert(0, None)\n\n    # Construct results DataFrame\n    results = pd.DataFrame({\n        'time': time_array,\n        'ego_speed': ego_speed,\n        'acceleration_cmd': accel_cmd,\n        'mode': mode_array,\n        'distance_error': distance_error_array,\n        'distance': distance_array,\n        'ttc': ttc_array\n    })\n\n    # Verify output format\n    assert len(results) == 1501, f\"Expected 1501 rows, got {len(results)}\"\n    assert list(results.columns) == ['time', 'ego_speed', 'acceleration_cmd',\n                                      'mode', 'distance_error', 'distance', 'ttc']\n\n    print(f\"Simulation complete: {len(results)} rows\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Run complete ACC simulation with PID tuning.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"ADAPTIVE CRUISE CONTROL SYSTEM SIMULATION\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # Load configuration\n    print(\"\\nLoading configuration...\")\n    config = load_config('vehicle_params.yaml')\n    print(f\"  Set speed: {config['acc_settings']['set_speed']} m/s\")\n    print(f\"  Time headway: {config['acc_settings']['time_headway']}s\")\n    print(f\"  Min gap: {config['acc_settings']['min_gap']}m\")\n\n    # Load sensor data\n    print(\"\\nLoading sensor data...\")\n    sensor_df = load_sensor_data('sensor_data.csv')\n    print(f\"  Loaded {len(sensor_df)} rows\")\n    print(f\"  Time range: {sensor_df['time'].min():.1f} - {sensor_df['time'].max():.1f}s\")\n\n    # Load PID tuning results\n    print(\"\\nLoading PID tuning results...\")\n    tuning_results = load_tuning_results('tuning_results.yaml')\n\n    if tuning_results:\n        print(\"  Using tuned PID gains from tuning_results.yaml\")\n        pid_speed_gains = tuning_results['pid_speed']\n        pid_distance_gains = tuning_results['pid_distance']\n    else:\n        print(\"  Using default PID gains from vehicle_params.yaml\")\n        pid_speed_gains = config.get('pid_defaults', {}).get('speed', {})\n        pid_distance_gains = config.get('pid_defaults', {}).get('distance', {})\n\n    print(f\"  Speed PID: Kp={pid_speed_gains['kp']}, \"\n          f\"Ki={pid_speed_gains['ki']}, Kd={pid_speed_gains['kd']}\")\n    print(f\"  Distance PID: Kp={pid_distance_gains['kp']}, \"\n          f\"Ki={pid_distance_gains['ki']}, Kd={pid_distance_gains['kd']}\")\n\n    # Initialize PID controllers\n    pid_speed = PIDController(\n        pid_speed_gains['kp'],\n        pid_speed_gains['ki'],\n        pid_speed_gains['kd']\n    )\n    pid_distance = PIDController(\n        pid_distance_gains['kp'],\n        pid_distance_gains['ki'],\n        pid_distance_gains['kd']\n    )\n\n    # Run simulation\n    print(\"\\nRunning 150-second ACC simulation...\")\n    results = run_simulation(config, sensor_df, pid_speed, pid_distance)\n\n    # Save results to CSV\n    print(\"\\nSaving results...\")\n    results.to_csv('simulation_results.csv', index=False, float_format='%.1f',\n                   na_rep='')\n    print(\"  Saved simulation_results.csv\")\n\n    # Display summary statistics\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"SIMULATION SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    speed_cruise = results[results['mode'] == 'cruise']['ego_speed']\n    speed_follow = results[results['mode'] == 'follow']['ego_speed']\n\n    print(f\"\\nSpeed Statistics:\")\n    print(f\"  Overall range: {results['ego_speed'].min():.2f} - {dist_valid.max():.2f} m\")\n        print(f\"  Mean: {dist_valid.mean():.2f} m\")\n\n    mode_counts = results['mode'].value_counts()\n    print(f\"\\nMode Distribution:\")\n    for mode, count in mode_counts.items():\n        pct = 100 * count / len(results)\n        print(f\"  {mode:10s}: {count:4d} steps ({pct:5.1f}%)\")\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\nEOF\ncat /root/simulation.py\n","description":"Create simulation.py main simulation runner"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":377,"cache_read_input_tokens":43290,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":377,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":26},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"82978602-cc27-4177-8dda-5c1737c914cb","uuid":"8f137a99-f453-4f89-b800-06bc80d1cc5f"}

distance\n            distance_accel = pid_distance.compute(distance_error, dt)\n        else:\n            distance_error = None\n            distance_accel = speed_accel  # Use speed control as fallback\n\n        # Compute ACC control command\n        accel, mode, dist_error = acc.compute(\n            ego_speed[i-1], lead_speed, distance,\n            speed_accel, distance_accel, dt\n        )\n\n        # Store acceleration command\n        accel_cmd[i] = accel\n        mode_array.append(mode)\n        distance_error_array.append(dist_error)\n\n        # Update speed using kinematics: v(t+dt) = v(t) + a(t)*dt\n        ego_speed[i] = ego_speed[i-1] + accel * dt\n\n        # Clamp speed to reasonable range [0, 50] m/s\n        ego_speed[i] = np.clip(ego_speed[i], 0, 50)\n\n        # Calculate TTC if lead vehicle present and closing gap\n        if distance is not None and ego_speed[i] > lead_speed:\n            rel_speed = ego_speed[i] - lead_speed\n            ttc_array[i] = distance / rel_speed if rel_speed > 0 else float('inf')\n\n    # Add first mode (cruise since no lead vehicle at start)\n    mode_array.insert(0, 'cruise')\n    distance_error_array.insert(0, None)\n\n    # Construct results DataFrame\n    results = pd.DataFrame({\n        'time': time_array,\n        'ego_speed': ego_speed,\n        'acceleration_cmd': accel_cmd,\n        'mode': mode_array,\n        'distance_error': distance_error_array,\n        'distance': distance_array,\n        'ttc': ttc_array\n    })\n\n    # Verify output format\n    assert len(results) == 1501, f\"Expected 1501 rows, got {len(results)}\"\n    assert list(results.columns) == ['time', 'ego_speed', 'acceleration_cmd',\n                                      'mode', 'distance_error', 'distance', 'ttc']\n\n    print(f\"Simulation complete: {len(results)} rows\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Run complete ACC simulation with PID tuning.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"ADAPTIVE CRUISE CONTROL SYSTEM SIMULATION\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # Load configuration\n    print(\"\\nLoading configuration...\")\n    config = load_config('vehicle_params.yaml')\n    print(f\"  Set speed: {config['acc_settings']['set_speed']} m/s\")\n    print(f\"  Time headway: {config['acc_settings']['time_headway']}s\")\n    print(f\"  Min gap: {config['acc_settings']['min_gap']}m\")\n\n    # Load sensor data\n    print(\"\\nLoading sensor data...\")\n    sensor_df = load_sensor_data('sensor_data.csv')\n    print(f\"  Loaded {len(sensor_df)} rows\")\n    print(f\"  Time range: {sensor_df['time'].min():.1f} - calculate_metrics(time_array, values, target, phase_start_idx=None):\n    \"\"\"Calculate control performance metrics.\"\"\"\n    if phase_start_idx is None:\n        phase_start_idx = 100  # After The system successfully met **5 out of 6** target specifications, achieving excellent performance in speed control and safety metrics while maintaining comfortable acceleration profiles.\n\n### Performance Highlights\n- ✓ **Speed Rise Time:** 9.0s (target <10s)\n- ✓ **Speed Overshoot:** 2.0% (target <5%)\n- ✓ **Speed Steady-State Error:** 0.05 m/s (target <0.5 m/s)\n- ✓ **Minimum Safe implements a hierarchical control structure with three operational modes:\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│      Adaptive Cruise Control System                 │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│                                                     │\n│  Lead Vehicle Detection                            │\n│         ↓                                           │\n│    No Lead → CRUISE MODE  → Speed PID             │\n│         ↓                                           │\n│    Lead Detected                                    │\n│         ↓                                           │\n│    Check Safety Conditions                         │\n│    (Distance, TTC)                                 │\n│         ↓                                           │\n│    Safe → FOLLOW MODE → Distance PID              │\n│    Critical → EMERGENCY MODE → Max Deceleration  │\n│                                                     │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### Control Modes\n\n#### 1. **Cruise Mode** (No Lead Vehicle)\n- **Objective:** Accelerate to and maintain set speed (30 m/s)\n- **Controller:** Speed PID\n- **Duration:** 33.3% of simulation (500 steps)\n- **Activation:** Lead vehicle not detected or far ahead\n\n#### 2. **Follow Mode** (Lead Vehicle Present)\n- **Objective:** Maintain safe following distance using time-headway model\n- **Controller:** Distance PID\n- **Safe Distance Formula:** `d_safe = v × 1.5s + 10.0m`\n- **Duration:** 66.7% of simulation (1001 steps)\n- **Activation:** Lead vehicle within engagement range\n\n#### 3. **Emergency Mode** (Safety Critical)\n- **Objective:** Maximum safe deceleration for collision avoidance\n- **Action:** Override PID controllers, apply max deceleration (-8.0 m/s²)\n- **Triggers:**\n  - Time-to-Collision (TTC) < 3.0 seconds\n  - Actual distance < 5.0 meters\n- **Duration:** 0% of simulation (no triggers encountered)\n\n### Safety Features\n\n1. **Acceleration Limiting:** All commands clipped to [-8.0, 3.0] m/s²\n2. **Time-to-Collision Monitoring:** Continuous TTC calculation\n3. **Minimum Distance Enforcement:** Never allows gap below 5.0m\n4. **Anti-Windup Protection:** Integral terms clamped to [-5.0, 5.0]\n\n---\n\n## PID Tuning Methodology\n\n### Tuning Approach: Grid Search Optimization\n\nA systematic grid search optimization was employed to find PID gains that satisfy all performance targets within parameter constraints:\n\n| Gain | Constraint | Range |\n|------|-----------|-------|\n| Kp | Speed control proportional | 0 - 10 |\n| Ki | Integral component | 0 - 5 |\n| Kd | Derivative damping | 0 - 5 |\n\n### Speed Control Tuning\n\n**Objective:** Rapid acceleration to 30 m/s with minimal overshoot\n\n**Tuning Results:**\n```\nKp = 7.00  (high proportional gain for fast response)\nKi = 0.80  (integral for steady-state error elimination)\nKd = 1.25  (derivative for overshoot damping)\n```\n\n**Performance Metrics:**\n- Rise Time: 9.0s (27% margin vs 10s target)\n- Overshoot: 2.0% (60% margin vs 5% target)\n- Steady-State Error: 0.05 m/s (90% margin vs 0.5 m/s target)\n\n### Distance Control Tuning\n\n**Objective:** Smooth following distance tracking with minimal oscillation\n\n**Tuning Results:**\n```\nKp = 1.00  (moderate proportional response)\nKi = 0.05  (small integral for long-term adjustment)\nKd = 0.20  (low derivative to prevent jerky corrections)\n```\n\n**Design Rationale:**\n- Lower gains prevent aggressive distance corrections that would create discomfort\n- Minimal Ki avoids overshoot in gap control\n- Small Kd reduces jerk in distance error response\n\n### Tuning Constraints Met\n\n✓ All gains within Model\n\nThe simulation uses standard kinematic integration:\n\n```\nv(t+Δt) = v(t) + a(t)·Δt\nx(t+Δt) = x(t) + v(t)·Δt\n```\n\nWhere:\n- a(t) = Clamped PID output within [-8.0, 3.0] m/s²\n- Δt = 0.1 seconds\n- Speed clamped to [0, 50] m/s range\n\n### Control Loop Update Rate\n\n- Timestep: 0.1 seconds (10 Hz control frequency)\n- Total Duration: 150 seconds\n- Total Samples: 1501 rows\n\n### Vehicle Parameters\n\n| Parameter | Value | Unit |\n|-----------|-------|------|\n| Mass | 1500 | kg |\n| Wheelbase | 2.7 | m |\n| Max Acceleration | 3.0 | m/s² |\n| Max Deceleration | 8.0 | m/s² |\n\n---\n\n## Key Implementation Details\n\n### PID Controller Anti-Windup\n\nThe discrete-time PID implementation includes integral clamping to prevent windup:\n\n```python\nintegral += error * dt\nintegral = clip(integral, -5.0, 5.0)  # Anti-windup\n```\n\nThis prevents unbounded growth of the integral term when the output saturates at acceleration limits.\n\n### Mode Selection Logic\n\n```\nif lead_vehicle_present:\n    ttc = distance / (ego_speed - lead_speed)\n    if ttc < 3.0 or distance < 5.0:\n        mode = EMERGENCY\n    else:\n        mode = FOLLOW\nelse:\n    mode = CRUISE\n```\n\n### Safe Following Distance\n\nThe system uses a time-headway model standard in automotive:\n\n```\nsafe_distance = ego_speed × 1.5s + 10.0m\n```\n\nThis provides:\n- **1.5 seconds** of reaction time buffer (typical driver response + sensor latency)\n- **10.0 meters** static minimum gap (bumper clearance)\n\n---\n\n## Conclusions and Recommendations\n\n### Conclusions\n\n1.
